Close is exposed only from RESULT_RECORDED. It is acceptable for controlled UAT, but amendments are needed before relying on it as a strong operational control.
Recommended amendments and benefit:
  1. Show due date, overdue days, escalation, owner, latest update, and evidence state: makes aged cases visible and accountable.
  2. Add an SLA overdue queue with notifications/escalation: reduces missed warehouse follow-up.
  3. Rename Findings to Unsellable Investigations and technical Exception #1 to a human related-discrepancy reference: clearer for operators.
  4. Add search, shop/reason/status/overdue filters, sorting, and pagination: makes the 86-row queue manageable.
  5. Hide or relabel zero-available Create case as Assigned: prevents misleading duplicate-action prompts.
  6. Require evidence for response/result/close at client and server: prevents unsupported closure and improves auditability.
  7. Add version, idempotency, correlation, actor, and before/after history controls: prevents duplicate/conflicting writes and makes every change traceable.
  8. Use validated selectors and automatic links for outbound/exception relationships: reduces wrong-ID linkage.
